# STATE FILE PATHS

# ────────
# CANONICAL STATE WRITE MECHANISM
# ────────
state_write_protocol:
  mechanism: "append-gateway"
  command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/append-mode-event.cjs --mode research --run-directory {artifact_dir} --event-json <record file>"
  applies_to: "every append_to_jsonl and append_jsonl directive in this workflow"
  refusal_handling: "halt on exit 2; never fall back to a direct file write. Exit 1 on a row that is still routed through the gateway is a workflow defect and halts; the pinned_bookkeeping rows are recorded through bookkeeping_log and are never routed."
  pinned_bookkeeping:
    events: [config_warning, graph_convergence, lock_released, manualStop, pivot_confirm_accepted, schema_mismatch, maxDurationReached]
    directive: "bookkeeping_log"
    handling: "These rows are workflow bookkeeping that the research ledger schema pins to the legacy path: they have no lossless research-event target, so the gateway would refuse them with exit 1 and write nothing. They are never sent to the gateway. The step prints the row in its log line and continues; none of them is a halt condition."
  contract: |
    The directives below specify WHAT to record; this block specifies the mechanism. The
    command accepts a canonical event envelope — a `stem` with `scope` and `data`, or an
    `event_type` with a `payload` — and for this mode it also accepts a legacy-shaped row,
    which it upcasts before appending. Where the upcast is lossy it returns a warning, and
    the warning is surfaced rather than swallowed. Some directives below record workflow
    bookkeeping rather than research semantics: the schema deliberately pins several of them
    to the legacy path and the upcaster has no registered stem for the rest. Those rows are
    declared under pinned_bookkeeping and recorded through bookkeeping_log, so they are never
    routed here. A refusal names its own reason, so the caller can tell a deliberate pin from
    a missing stem. A row the upcaster does refuse returns exit 1 and writes nothing, with the
    reason naming why. Exit 1 is distinct from the exit 2 refusal described in the next
    paragraph: exit 1 means the input never reached authority, exit 2 means authority refused
    it.

    The gateway authorizes the write against the mode's durable authority record, serialises it
    behind the ledger fence, returns a receipt, and refreshes state_log from the ledger. Exit 0
    means the record is durable. Exit 2 means it was refused, and the refusal names the check
    that failed.

    Writing state_log directly bypasses ordering, authorization, and the receipt. Two writers
    can then interleave with no shared ordering, and a record nobody authorized carries no
    receipt, so a later reader cannot tell an accepted record from an invented one. A refusal
    is a halt, never a reason to write the file directly.

    There is no direct-append exception. The run-now sentinels, the restored-sentinel row and
    the synthesis reconciliation rows are canonical ledger events, because state_log is a
    projection the gateway rebuilds from the ledger and a row written beside it is dropped by
    the next refresh.

state_paths:
  # Template paths document the canonical packet layout. Runtime resolution happens via
  # step_resolve_artifact_root plus the shared resolver, so root specs keep root-local
  # research/ and child phases keep local research/ packet directories.
  packet_dir: "{artifact_dir}"
  config: "{artifact_dir}/deep-research-config.json"
  state_log: "{artifact_dir}/deep-research-state.jsonl"
  status_ledger: "{artifact_dir}/orchestration-status.log"
  strategy: "{artifact_dir}/deep-research-strategy.md"
  registry: "{artifact_dir}/findings-registry.json"
  dashboard: "{artifact_dir}/deep-research-dashboard.md"
  pause_sentinel: "{artifact_dir}/.deep-research-pause"
  run_now_sentinel: "{artifact_dir}/.deep-research-run-now"
  lock_file: "{artifact_dir}/.deep-research.lock"
  prompt_dir: "{artifact_dir}/prompts"
  iteration_dir: "{artifact_dir}/iterations"
  iteration_pattern: "{artifact_dir}/iterations/iteration-{NNN}.md"
  # Per-iteration structured delta stream (one file per iteration) — complement to the
  # canonical state_log append. The delta file MUST contain at least one record whose
  # `type === "iteration"` so the reducer can rehydrate iteration state after a partial
  # run. Variants like `"iteration_delta"` are silently dropped by the reducer; the
  # post_dispatch_validate step enforces the canonical type below.
  delta_dir: "{artifact_dir}/deltas"
  delta_pattern: "{artifact_dir}/deltas/iter-{NNN}.jsonl"
  research_output: "{artifact_dir}/research.md"
  resource_map_output: "{artifact_dir}/resource-map.md"
  archive_root: "{artifact_archive_root}"
  synthesis_snapshot_pattern: "{artifact_dir}/synthesis-v{generation}.md"

# ────────
# WORKFLOW
# ────────

gate_defaults:
  after_gate_refresh_lock:
    action: "Refresh the packet lock heartbeat immediately after the operator response"
    command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs refresh --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}"
    on_refresh: "If the refresh output has refreshed:false, fail closed: lost the packet lock. This run no longer holds the packet, so run step_release_lock and stop rather than continue without it."

workflow:

  step_resolve_artifact_root:
    description: "Resolve artifact directory — use fan-out lineage override when provided, else resolve from spec_folder"
    note: "Root specs keep research artifacts directly in {spec_folder}/research/. Child phases and sub-phases keep local packet directories in {spec_folder}/research/{packet}/, and archives stay local at {spec_folder}/research_archive/{packet}/."
    branch_on: "config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir"
    if_present:
      bind:
        artifact_dir: "{config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir}"
        artifact_root_dir: "{config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir}"
        artifact_subfolder: null
        artifact_archive_root: "{config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir}/_archive"
    if_absent:
      command: "node -e \"const { resolveArtifactRoot } = require('./.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/shared/review-research-paths.cjs'); console.log(JSON.stringify(resolveArtifactRoot('{spec_folder}', 'research')));\""
      outputs:
        - artifact_root_dir: "Resolved local research owner directory"
        - artifact_subfolder: "Local packet subfolder ({ownerSlug}-pt-{NN}), else null"
        - artifact_dir: "Resolved research packet directory for this target"
        - artifact_archive_root: "Resolved local research archive directory for this target"

  step_bind_reducer_artifact_arg:
    description: "Keep the reducer on the lineage-owned artifact directory during fan-out"
    branch_on: "config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir"
    if_present:
      bind:
        reducer_artifact_arg: "--artifact-dir {artifact_dir}"
    if_absent:
      bind:
        reducer_artifact_arg: ""

  step_apply_lifecycle_request:
    description: "Honor an explicit restart request before fan-out or normal initialization"
    dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
    skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'restart'"
    instructions: |
      Run this step in both execution modes, immediately after artifact resolution
      and before fan-out, so old packet files cannot be merged with fresh lineage
      outputs. A restart request must never fall back to resume. The explicit
      restart flag is operator authorization to archive the resolved research packet,
      so do not ask for a second confirmation. Rollback is to move the timestamped
      archive back to the packet directory.
    bind_prior_lineage:
      action: "Read the session id and generation of the packet that is about to be archived"
      command: |
        node -e '
          const fs = require("fs");
          const crypto = require("crypto");
          let priorSessionId = null;
          let priorGeneration = 0;
          try {
            const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[1], "utf8"));
            const lineage = config.lineage || config;
            priorSessionId = typeof lineage.sessionId === "string" ? lineage.sessionId : null;
            priorGeneration = Number(lineage.generation) || 0;
          } catch (error) {
            if (error.code !== "ENOENT") {
              console.error("The old config cannot be read: " + error.message);
              process.exit(1);
            }
          }
          let priorTailDigest = crypto.createHash("sha256").update("").digest("hex");
          try {
            priorTailDigest = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2])).digest("hex");
          } catch (error) {
            if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
          }
          console.log(JSON.stringify({ priorSessionId, priorGeneration, priorTailDigest }));
        ' "{state_paths.config}" "{state_paths.state_log}"
      outputs:
        - prior_session_id: "priorSessionId from the JSON output, null when no old config exists"
        - prior_generation: "priorGeneration from the JSON output, 0 when no old config exists"
        - prior_tail_digest: "priorTailDigest from the JSON output: the sha256 of the old state log bytes, or of zero bytes when no old state log exists"
      on_fail: "Halt with STATUS=FAIL. The old config is unreadable, so repair or archive the packet by hand."
    command: |
      if [ -e "{state_paths.archive_root}/{timestamp_slug}" ]; then
        echo "The archive folder {state_paths.archive_root}/{timestamp_slug} is already there." >&2
        exit 1
      fi
      if [ -d "{state_paths.packet_dir}" ]; then
        mkdir -p "{state_paths.archive_root}" && mv "{state_paths.packet_dir}" "{state_paths.archive_root}/{timestamp_slug}" || exit 1
      fi
      mkdir -p "{state_paths.packet_dir}"
    outputs:
      - archived_path: "{state_paths.archive_root}/{timestamp_slug} when a prior packet existed, else null"
      - lineage_mode: "restart"
    on_fail: "Halt with STATUS=FAIL; do not continue as resume or auto."

  step_reject_extend_with_fanout:
    description: "Refuse --extend on a fan-out config before any lineage spawns"
    skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'completed-continue' or config.fanout is absent"
    instructions: |
      A fan-out run jumps from step_fanout_spawn to phase_synthesis and never
      reaches the lock, the pre-classify extension step or the classifier, so an
      extension there would change nothing and report no error. Refuse the
      combination here, before step_fanout_spawn, so no lineage spawns.
    halt: true
    status: "FAIL"
    message: "Usage error (extend-with-fanout): --extend extends a single-executor research run only. This config has fanout executors, so nothing was spawned and the run is unchanged."

  step_frontier_seed:
    description: "Seed the depth-1 frontier before any fan-out lineage spawns"
    enabled_when: "depth_mode == 'expansive'"
    skip_when: "config.fanout is absent"
    dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
    instructions: |
      Fan-out jumps from step_fanout_spawn to phase_synthesis and never reaches
      phase_init, so the seed runs here or not at all. fanout-run.cjs then splits
      the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across the lineages.
      Decompose {research_topic} into 3 to 6 depth-1 sub-questions that together
      cover the topic. Write them as a JSON array to {artifact_dir}/frontier-seed.json,
      one object per sub-question: {"question": "...", "scope": "<path, module or
      area>", "evidence": "<repo path or topic phrase that grounds it>"}. Then run
      the command. frontier-admit.cjs is the only writer of the node store and the
      frontier inbox rows; never write either file by hand.
    command: 'node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --artifact-dir "{artifact_dir}" --iteration 0 --input "{artifact_dir}/frontier-seed.json"'
    working_directory: "{repo_root}"
    outputs:
      - frontier_seed_json: "The JSON line the command prints: admitted node ids and pruned {nodeId, reason} rows"
    on_failure: "Halt with the command's stderr line. A run that asked for expansive depth must not start without its seeded frontier."

  step_fanout_spawn:
    description: "Spawn fan-out lineages; skip and proceed to phase_init normally when single-executor"
    skip_when: "config.fanout is absent"
    dry_run_boundary: "child-spawn"
    instructions: |
      Fan-out mode is active. Spawn all lineages. Each lineage runs the full loop in its own
      isolated sub-packet under {artifact_dir}/lineages/{label}/.
      fanout-run.cjs owns native and CLI lineage execution; native is run through the
      deep/research command surface with config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir so the LEAF
      deep-research agent is used only for individual iterations, never as a full-loop
      sub-agent or pasted phase-running prompt.
      After all lineages complete, jump directly to phase_synthesis — skip phase_init and
      phase_main_loop entirely (those are the single-executor path). The merge step in
      phase_synthesis will consolidate results across all lineages.
    sub_steps:
      step_fanout_spawn_cli:
        description: "Spawn all native and CLI lineages in capped worker pool"
        skip_when: "no entries in config.fanout.executors"
        command: |

            --research-topic "{research_topic}" \

            --stop-policy {stop_policy} \
            --convergence-mode {convergence_mode} \
            --depth-mode {depth_mode}
        working_directory: "{repo_root}"
        wait_for_completion: true
        optional_flags: "When lineage_timeout_hours is bound, append `--lineage-timeout-hours {lineage_timeout_hours}` to the command. When it is unset, append nothing, which keeps the 4 hour ceiling."

on_halt:
          action: "Every terminal halt after the acquire runs step_release_lock before it returns control"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; failures here are logged but do not block the halt"

      step_extend_completed_run:
        action: "Extend a completed run in place, under the lock and before classification, so the classifier sees a resumable run"
        skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'completed-continue'"
        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
        note: |
          --extend=N continues a finished run in the same session and generation. The
          duration clock counts wall-clock time since createdAt, idle time included, so the
          new budget starts from whichever is later, the old budget or the time already
          spent, and createdAt is never re-stamped. The event is appended before the
          config is replaced, so a failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.
        command: |
          GATEWAY=".skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
          [ -f "$GATEWAY" ] || GATEWAY="scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
          WORK_DIR="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/deep-research-extend.XXXXXX")"
          node -e '
            const fs = require("fs"), crypto = require("crypto"), path = require("path");
            const [configPath, stateLogPath, inboxPath, extendRaw, extendMinutesRaw, workDir] = process.argv.slice(1);
            const fail = (message) => { console.error(message); process.exit(2); };
            const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
            // The preflight check refuses these first; this keeps a bad value from reaching the config.
            if (!/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendRaw))) {
              fail("--extend needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(String(extendRaw)) + ".");
            }
            const minutesRaw = String(extendMinutesRaw);
            if (minutesRaw !== "" && !minutesRaw.startsWith("{") && !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(minutesRaw)) {
              fail("--extend-minutes needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(minutesRaw) + ".");
            }
            if (config.status !== "complete") {
              fail("--extend needs a completed run, and this run has status " + config.status + ". Use --lineage-mode=resume to continue an unfinished run.");
            }
            const stateBytes = fs.readFileSync(stateLogPath);
            const rows = stateBytes.toString("utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean).flatMap((line) => {
              try { return [JSON.parse(line)]; } catch { return []; }
            });
            // One run per iteration number, error and timeout rows included. The integer
            // iteration counts first and a numeric run is the fallback, as the state reader
            // counts them, because a leaf may write its run id string in run.
            const continuedFromRun = new Set(rows
              .filter((row) => row && row.type === "iteration")
              .map((row) => (Number.isInteger(row.iteration) ? row.iteration : Number(row.run)))
              .filter((run) => Number.isInteger(run))).size;
            const addedIterations = Number(extendRaw);
            const priorMaxIterations = Number(config.maxIterations) || 0;
            const priorMaxDurationMinutes = Number(config.maxDurationMinutes) || 0;
            const createdMs = Date.parse(config.createdAt);
            const elapsedMinutesAtExtension = Number.isFinite(createdMs)
              ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - createdMs) / 60000))
              : 0;
            // An unset flag renders as an empty or unexpanded value, never as digits.
            const hasMinutes = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendMinutesRaw));
            const addedMinutes = hasMinutes ? Number(extendMinutesRaw) : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            // A run with no duration budget keeps none unless --extend-minutes asks for one.
            const newMaxDurationMinutes = priorMaxDurationMinutes > 0 || hasMinutes
              ? Math.max(priorMaxDurationMinutes, elapsedMinutesAtExtension) + addedMinutes
              : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            const newMaxIterations = continuedFromRun + addedIterations;
            const lineage = config.lineage || {};
            const sessionId = lineage.sessionId;
            const lineageId = lineage.parentSessionId || sessionId;
            const generation = Number(lineage.generation) || 1;
            const event = {
              stem: "deep_research.run_extended",
              scope: { runId: sessionId, lineageId },
              data: {
                priorTailDigest: crypto.createHash("sha256").update(stateBytes).digest("hex"),
                sessionId,
                generation,
                continuedFromRun,
                extension: {
                  addedIterations,
                  priorMaxIterations,
                  newMaxIterations,
                  priorMaxDurationMinutes,
                  newMaxDurationMinutes,
                  elapsedMinutesAtExtension,
                },
              },
            };
            const next = {
              ...config,
              status: "initialized",
              maxIterations: newMaxIterations,
              maxDurationMinutes: newMaxDurationMinutes,
              lineage: { ...lineage, lineageMode: "completed-continue", continuedFromRun },
            };
            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "event.json"), JSON.stringify(event));
            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "config.json"), JSON.stringify(next, null, 2) + "\n");
            let pendingInbox = 0;
            try {
              pendingInbox = fs.readFileSync(inboxPath, "utf8").split("\n").filter((line) => line.trim() !== "").length;
            } catch (error) {
              if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
            }
            const stopPolicy = config.antiConvergence && config.antiConvergence.stopPolicy || "convergence";
            const warning = stopPolicy === "convergence" && pendingInbox === 0
              ? "This run stops on convergence and has no pending inbox question, so it may re-stop on all_questions_answered right away. Pass --convergence-mode=off, use stopPolicy max-iterations, or add questions to research/inbox.jsonl to keep it running."

# ────────
# DEEP REVIEW: DEEP REVIEW LOOP (AUTO AND CONFIRM MODES)
# ────────
# DEEP REVIEW: DEEP REVIEW LOOP (AUTO AND CONFIRM MODES)
# ────────
role: Deep Review Loop Manager
purpose: Iterative code/spec review with convergence detection across review dimensions
action: Run deep-review loop from initialization through synthesis
operating_mode:
  workflow: iterative_loop
  workflow_compliance: MANDATORY
  workflow_execution: autonomous
  approvals: none
  tracking: progressive_review_state
  validation: convergence_detection

# ────────
# OPERATING PRINCIPLES
# ────────
operating_principles:
  core: "Fresh context per iteration + externalized state = no context degradation"
  approach: "Dispatch focused review agent per dimension, evaluate convergence, repeat or stop"
  mandate: "Never hold findings in memory; write everything to files. Review target is READ-ONLY."

# ────────
# EXECUTION MODE
# ────────
execution_mode:
  source: "$ARGUMENTS"
  values: [auto, confirm]
  default: confirm
  rule: "Every step, executor branch and flag in this file runs in both modes. Under confirm, also execute every block marked applies_to: confirm at the place that carries it, and apply the mode_overrides.confirm wording. Under auto, skip every block marked applies_to: confirm and keep the autonomous wording of this file."
mode_overrides:
  confirm:
    purpose: "Iterative code/spec review with convergence detection and user approval gates"
    action: "Run deep-review loop with user confirmation at each phase transition"
    workflow_execution: "interactive"
    approvals: "multi_gate"
    approach: "Dispatch focused review agent per dimension, show results, get approval, repeat or stop"
    mandate: "Never hold findings in memory; write everything to files. Pause for user at each gate. Review target is READ-ONLY."
    phase_loop_purpose: "Dispatch review agent iterations with approval at each step"
    lock_not_acquired: "If acquired=false, stop and present recovery choices: another deep-review run already holds this packet."
    agent_dispatch_timeout: "Retry once with reduced scope. If second timeout, mark as 'timeout' and show to user for decision."
    continuity_save_failure: "Preserve the review packet in review/ as backup. Inform user."
    rules_always: [pause_at_approval_gates, present_clear_options_at_each_gate]
    rules_never: [proceed_past_approval_gate_without_user_response, auto_approve_on_behalf_of_user]

# ────────
# USER INPUTS (from setup phase)
# ────────
user_inputs:
  review_target: "[REVIEW_TARGET] - What to review. REQUIRED."
  review_target_type: "[spec-folder|skill|agent|track|files] - Target type for scope derivation. REQUIRED."
  review_dimensions: "[all|correctness,security,traceability,maintainability|custom names] - Dimensions to check. The four canonical dimensions are correctness, security, traceability and maintainability; any other name is a custom dimension, reviewed after the canonical four in configured order. Default: all."
  review_focus: "[optional free text] - Operator review focus that every iteration covers in addition to its dimension. Default: empty, which leaves the prompt unchanged."
  spec_folder: "[SPEC_FOLDER] - Spec folder path for state files. REQUIRED."
  session_id: "[SESSION_ID] - Optional caller-supplied session id for fan-out lineages. Default: timestamp."
  lineage_mode: "[auto|resume|restart|completed-continue] - Session lifecycle intent. Default: auto. The runtime persists lineage events for `resume` (same sessionId, no archive), `restart` (new sessionId, generation+1, prior review/ archived) and `completed-continue` (from --extend=N: a completed run extended in place by N iterations, same sessionId and generation). `fork` is not accepted. See deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md §Lifecycle Branches."
  extend: "[EXTEND] - Positive integer N from --extend=N. Sets lineage_mode to completed-continue and adds N iterations to a completed run. Default: unset."
  extend_minutes: "[EXTEND_MINUTES] - Positive integer M from --extend-minutes=M, the minutes added to the duration budget on an extension. Only valid with --extend. Default: unset, which adds the prior maxDurationMinutes."
  max_iterations: 7
  convergence_threshold: 0.10
  convergence_mode: "[default|off|sliding-window|divergent] - Convergence policy modifier. Default: default."
  depth_mode: "[default|expansive] - Opt-in frontier growth. expansive seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes, admits leaf frontier_proposal rows and vetoes a review stop candidate while nodes stay open. Default: default."
  stop_policy: "[convergence|max-iterations] - Stop behavior. Default: convergence. max-iterations treats convergence as telemetry until the hard iteration ceiling."
  dry_run: "[DRY_RUN] - Boolean pre-flight flag. Default: false."

dry_run_control:
  enabled_when: "dry_run == true"
  default: false
  modes: [auto, confirm]
  purpose: "Run safe setup and read-only inspection, then stop before side effects."
  safe_operations:
    - "Resolve setup inputs and artifact roots."
    - "Read current state, strategy, focus, and convergence signals."
    - "Render the next prompt to terminal preview without writing prompt files."
  forbidden_operations:
    - "Executor dispatch."
    - "Persistent state, queue, lock, git index, memory, or graph mutation."
    - "Reducer refresh or generated artifact writes."
    - "Child-lineage spawn."
  event_policy:
    event_name: dry_run_halt
    sink: "terminal JSONL preview only; never append to {state_paths.state_log}"
    required_fields: [type, event, mode, boundary, step, timestamp]
    optional_fields: [run, sessionId, generation, detail]
    default_halt_event: '{"type":"event","event":"dry_run_halt","mode":"review","boundary":"{dry_run_boundary}","step":"{step_name}","timestamp":"{ISO_8601_NOW}"}'
    preview_only: "A step or branch marked dry_run_event_policy: preview-only still runs its read and classify work. It prints the event it would append as a terminal JSONL preview line and appends nothing."
  halt_hook_rule: "Before executing any command, dispatch, edit, output, template write, append_to_jsonl, append_jsonl, log_event, mcp_tool, write, or git-index mutation in a step tagged with dry_run_boundary, emit event_policy.default_halt_event with that step's name and boundary to the terminal and halt. This holds in both execution modes."
  boundary_labels:
    child_spawn: "child-spawn"
    state_mutation: "state-mutation"
    dispatch: "dispatch"
    reducer_refresh: "reducer-refresh"

context_loading:
  trigger: "At workflow START, before phase_init"
  purpose: "Load prior review context for the target and buffer it until strategy.md exists"
  retrieval:
    mechanism: "continuity-ladder-then-ripgrep"
    note: "File reads only. Nothing here needs a running daemon, and every step is reproducible by hand."
    ladder:
      - "{spec_folder}/handover.md when present"
      - "the _memory.continuity frontmatter block in {spec_folder}/implementation-summary.md"
      - "the packet's canonical spec docs, spec.md and decision-record.md, for what the first two steps leave open"
    free_text_recipe: "rg --no-config --json --fixed-strings --ignore-case -C 2 --glob '*.md' --glob '!**/z_archive/**' --glob '!**/node_modules/**' -- '{review_target}' specs .skilled"
    recipe_contract: ".skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/retrieval/retrieval-conventions.md"
    exit_status: "0 is a hit set, 1 is a clean no-hit, 2 or higher is a failure that must be surfaced instead of reported as no-hit"
    ranking: "Ripgrep does not rank. Order the hits caller-side with the tuple in retrieval-conventions.md section 5."
  when_to_skip: ["User says 'skip context' or 'fresh start'"]
  behavior:
    if_context_found: "Store full results as prior_context plus a concise prior_context_summary; inject after strategy.md is created."

      step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the review config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out thought iterations and every iteration whose status is error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap is evaluated before every veto, the claim-adjudication veto included; failed legal-stop gates are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every dimension is clean (all_dimensions_clean) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged). The vote cutoffs follow the configured convergenceThreshold.
          4a. if iteration_count < min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached). min_iterations is config.minIterations, else antiConvergence.minIterations, capped at max_iterations; like research, the floor runs on a candidate before the legal-stop gates.
          5. the nine legal-stop gates run on a candidate: convergenceGate, dimensionCoverageGate, p0ResolutionGate, evidenceDensityGate, hotspotSaturationGate, claimAdjudicationGate, fixCompletenessReplayGate, candidateCoverageGate, graphlessFallbackGate. Any failed gate gives BLOCKED (blockedStop).
          6. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision, where G is stop_decision.gates and I is stop_decision.inputs.review:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            stopReason = stop_decision.stopReason
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            convergence_gate_pass = G.convergenceGate.pass
            convergence_gate_score = G.convergenceGate.score
            dimension_coverage_gate_pass = G.dimensionCoverageGate.pass
            dimension_coverage_gate_missing_json = G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing
            dimension_coverage_gate_covered_json = the configured review dimensions that are not in G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing
            p0_resolution_gate_pass = G.p0ResolutionGate.pass
            active_p0_count = G.p0ResolutionGate.activeP0
            evidence_density_gate_pass = G.evidenceDensityGate.pass
            avg_evidence_per_finding = I.evidenceBackedP0P1, the count of active P0 and P1 findings that carry file:line evidence
            hotspot_saturation_gate_pass = G.hotspotSaturationGate.pass
            claim_adjudication_gate_pass = G.claimAdjudicationGate.pass
            claim_adjudication_active_count = I.activeP0 plus I.activeP1
            fix_completeness_replay_gate_pass = G.fixCompletenessReplayGate.pass
            security_sensitive_fix_scope = I.securitySensitiveFixScope
            fix_completeness_replay_required_rows = I.fixReplayRequiredRows

        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"

          rebuild, and the projection refuses to drop them, so every later append in the run would

// Optional operator review focus, carried into every review lineage beside its dimensions.
  const reviewFocus = normalizeReviewFocus(args.reviewFocus);
  if (reviewFocus && loopType !== 'review') {
    throw inputError('reviewFocus applies only to the review loop type');
  }
  if ((args.reviewTarget || args.reviewTargetType) && loopType !== 'review') {
    throw inputError('reviewTarget applies only to the review loop type');
  }
  const reviewTargetBinding = resolveReviewTarget(args.reviewTarget, args.reviewTargetType, specFolder);
  const fanoutConfigJson = ensureString(args, 'fanoutConfigJson');
  const baseArtifactDir = validateBaseArtifactDir(ensureString(args, 'baseArtifactDir'), specFolder, loopType);
  const convergenceThreshold = parseOptionalNumber(args, 'convergenceThreshold');
  const lineageTimeoutHoursOverride = parseOptionalNumber(args, 'lineageTimeoutHours');
  assertLineageTimeoutHoursOverrideWithinCeiling(lineageTimeoutHoursOverride);
  const stopPolicy = normalizeStopPolicy(args.stopPolicy);
  const convergenceMode = normalizeConvergenceMode(args.convergenceMode);
  const depthMode = normalizeDepthMode(args.depthMode);
  const containmentModeOverride = normalizeContainmentMode(args.containmentMode);

.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:96:| `--convergence-mode` | `default` \| `off` \| `sliding-window` \| `divergent` | `default` keeps the anti-convergence `minIterations` floor. `off` disables convergence-driven STOP while max-iterations, pause and halt stay active. `sliding-window` scores novelty over a bounded window, five snapshots by default. `divergent` routes an eligible convergence stop into a Council pivot rather than ending the run. |
.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:97:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1 (record the pilot range SHA; merges are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff` and index rebuilds as `generated-artifact`), admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, holds open nodes as `searchDebt`, dispatches the active node's next uncovered dimension, and turns a review stop candidate into another iteration while nodes stay open. A node needs one iteration per configured dimension, so set `--max-iterations` to at least `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions` for the depth-1 band. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |
.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:104:| `--extend` | positive integer, as `--extend=N` | Extends a completed run in place by `N` iterations, lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event and keeps the prior review report as `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. The run must be complete, so use `--lineage-mode=resume` for an unfinished one. Refused with fan-out. Under `stopPolicy: convergence` an extension with nothing new can re-stop on `all_dimensions_clean` at once: pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`. |
.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:105:| `--extend-minutes` | positive integer, as `--extend-minutes=M` | Minutes added to the duration budget on an extension, default the prior `maxDurationMinutes`. The new budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts idle time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs. Valid only with `--extend`. |

.skilled/commands/deep/research.md:103:| `--convergence-mode` | `default` \| `off` \| `sliding-window` \| `divergent` | `default` keeps the anti-convergence `minIterations` floor. `off` disables convergence-driven STOP while max-iterations, pause and halt stay active. `sliding-window` scores novelty over a bounded window, five snapshots by default, on the ordinary legal-stop path. `divergent` sends a `composite_converged` or `all_questions_answered` stop into a one-round three-seat Council pivot instead of ending the run, at proportionally higher cost. |
.skilled/commands/deep/research.md:104:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 frontier nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as new questions under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), steers Next Focus to the shallowest open node, and turns a convergence stop into another iteration while frontier nodes stay open. == .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/state/state-format.md
79:### Frontier Node Store (expansive depth only)
81:`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row for a node wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit. `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` is the only writer.
83:Statuses are `open`, `active`, `answered`, `blocked`, `exhausted`, `abstained` and `pruned`. A frontier question resolves when its node reaches a terminal status.
85:The reducer projects the store as `registry.frontier {nodes, stats, budget}`, applies the depth budget so an unspent share rolls down to the next depth only, closes a spent band's open nodes as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and adds a `## 6B. FRONTIER` dashboard section.
153:The projection folds only registered stems, and its `run_initialized` arm rebuilds a thin config row: `type`, `topic`, `maxIterations`, `generation`, `timestamp`. A pre-flip config row written by the workflow carries more — `convergenceThreshold`, `minIterations`, `minIdeaObservations`, `antiConvergence`, `sessionId`, `executor`, `specFolder`, `createdAt` — and a refresh cannot rebuild those keys.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md
121:The runtime supports four lineage modes. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N`, which reopens a completed run in the same session and generation.
129:| `completed-continue` | same | same | none; the prior synthesis is copied to `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` | `extended` (see below) | a completed run needs N more iterations: pass `--extend=N` |
133:`--extend=N` (and optionally `--extend-minutes=M`) continues a run whose config has `status: "complete"`. Under the loop lock and before session classification, `step_extend_completed_run`:
137:3. Sets `maxDurationMinutes` to `max(prior budget, minutes elapsed since createdAt) + M`. `M` defaults to the prior budget, and a run with no duration budget keeps none unless `--extend-minutes` is passed. `createdAt` is never re-stamped, because the duration check counts wall-clock time, idle time included.
140:Classification then lands on resume. The workflow writes no `resumed` row for an extension, and the reducers treat an `extended` row like `resumed`, so the run is live again. Before the first new dispatch, `step_snapshot_extended_synthesis` copies the prior synthesis to `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` and refuses to overwrite an existing snapshot, so a second extension of the same generation keeps its own copy. `--extend` refuses a run that is not complete (use `--lineage-mode=resume`), refuses fan-out before any lineage spawns, and accepts positive integers only.
411:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of follow-up questions beside the key questions seeded at init. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 per depth, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. A run without the flag keeps the default path and renders the same bytes as before.
413:`step_frontier_seed` decomposes the topic into 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1. On a plain run it sits in `phase_init` after `step_create_config`; on a fan-out run it runs before `step_fanout_spawn`. It calls `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`.
415:A leaf may append `frontier_proposal` rows to its delta only when its prompt carries a FRONTIER block, which `frontier-admit.cjs block` computes from the node's remaining child slots and the prompt pack renders through `{frontier_block}`. `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and calls `frontier-admit.cjs admit`, which applies six checks in order: relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget, cycle. The first failure prunes the proposal with that reason. Admitted nodes become `research/inbox.jsonl` rows with `origin: "frontier"`.
419:Under stuck recovery the handler closes the focused node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck` through `frontier-admit.cjs close`, keeps the `stuckRecovery` event, and moves to the next open node. Global recovery runs only when no node is open.
421:On a fan-out run, `fanout-run.cjs --depth-mode expansive` deals the seeded open depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order across lineages, writes each lineage's `frontier-nodes.jsonl` and inbox rows before it spawns, binds `frontier_scope:` in the lineage prompt and records `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. `fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers, each node keeping its lineage label, and records a partition guard that names any dealt node no lineage reports.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/convergence/convergence.md
68:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a frontier of follow-up questions beside the questions seeded at init. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, `budgetSplit` 0.5/0.35/0.15 for depths 1, 2 and 3, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before; `research-depth-mode-golden.vitest` proves it.
70:After the `minIterations` floor, an expansive run with open or active frontier nodes turns a soft stop candidate into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Under `--convergence-mode=divergent` the pivot becomes eligible only once no frontier node is open or active.
76:Research pivots are eligible only when the workflow decision is `STOP` and the exact internal reason is `composite_converged` or `all_questions_answered`. The legal-stop and quality gates run before this branch. `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `stuckRecovery`, `minIterationsNotReached`, `error`, `manualStop`, and `userPaused` are explicitly excluded. Max iterations, pause or cancellation, manual stop, and unrecoverable error therefore never dispatch a pivot Council.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/guides/quick-reference.md
59:| `--depth-mode` | default | `expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions and vetoes a convergence stop while nodes stay open |
106:> **Live lifecycle branches:** `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue` through `--extend=N`.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md
91:- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions beside the seeded key questions. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before.
92:- `step_frontier_seed` writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`, after `step_create_config`, or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run.
93:- `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Admitted nodes land in `research/inbox.jsonl` with `origin: "frontier"`.
95:- After the `minIterations` floor an open or active node turns a soft stop into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never creates a stop and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`.
135:`completed-continue` is set by `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session and generation, raises the iteration cap to the runs so far plus N, and appends a typed `run_extended` event.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md
127:`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.
199:A: `resume` keeps the same `sessionId` and appends a `resumed` event. `restart` archives the current `research/` tree under `research_archive/`, mints a fresh `sessionId`, increments `generation` and appends a `restarted` event. `completed-continue` comes from `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session, raises the cap to the runs so far plus N, snapshots the prior synthesis and appends an `extended` event.
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/deep-research-strategy.md
130:<!-- ANCHOR:divergence-frontier -->
137:- Remaining frontier: none recorded
138:<!-- /ANCHOR:divergence-frontier -->
179:- Lifecycle branches: `resume`, `restart`, `completed-continue` through `--extend=N` (live)
== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md
199:### Completed-run extension (--extend)
203:A completed deep-research run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and records one run_extended event.
207:The run continues under lineage mode `completed-continue`, keeps the prior synthesis as a snapshot and takes `--extend-minutes=M` on the duration budget. Extension is refused with fan-out.
391:The config template defines the anti-convergence defaults and the workflow reads them before accepting a convergence STOP. A STOP candidate is overridden while `iterationCount < minIterations`; when the floor clears, the workflow emits `min_iterations_guard_pass`. `convergenceMode: "off"` disables convergence STOP while preserving hard caps, pause, and halt behavior.
463:### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)
467:Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-research seeds depth-one frontier nodes from the topic, admits proposals under caps, and keeps a convergence stop open while frontier nodes stay open.
471:`default` is the value when the flag is absent and runs unchanged. `frontier-admit.cjs` admits proposals into the node store, and the iteration cap, the floor, stuck recovery, pause, convergence off and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.
475:See [`convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '480,600p' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml
# Lock-cleanup contract: the lock file MUST be released on every terminal exit path that follows acquisition.
        # Runtimes honoring step_acquire_lock are required to perform this cleanup before halting or pausing.
        on_halt:
          action: "Every terminal halt after the acquire runs step_release_lock before it returns control"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; failures here are logged but do not block the halt"
        on_cancel:
          action: "Release the lock on user cancel or pause-sentinel detection before sentinel processing"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; cancel completes regardless of cleanup result"
        on_workflow_exit:
          action: "Release the lock as the final step of any workflow exit path (success or failure)"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; idempotent — safe to invoke when already released"

      step_extend_completed_run:
        action: "Extend a completed run in place, under the lock and before classification, so the classifier sees a resumable run"
        skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'completed-continue'"
        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
        note: |
          --extend=N continues a finished run in the same session and generation. The
          duration clock counts wall-clock time since createdAt, idle time included, so the
          new budget starts from whichever is later, the old budget or the time already
          spent, and createdAt is never re-stamped. The event is appended before the
          config is replaced, so a failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.
        command: |
          GATEWAY=".skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
          [ -f "$GATEWAY" ] || GATEWAY="scripts/append-mode-event.cjs"
          WORK_DIR="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/deep-review-extend.XXXXXX")"
          node -e '
            const fs = require("fs"), crypto = require("crypto"), path = require("path");
            const [configPath, stateLogPath, extendRaw, extendMinutesRaw, workDir] = process.argv.slice(1);
            const fail = (message) => { console.error(message); process.exit(2); };
            const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
            // The preflight check refuses these first; this keeps a bad value from reaching the config.
            if (!/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendRaw))) {
              fail("--extend needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(String(extendRaw)) + ".");
            }
            const minutesRaw = String(extendMinutesRaw);
            if (minutesRaw !== "" && !minutesRaw.startsWith("{") && !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(minutesRaw)) {
              fail("--extend-minutes needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(minutesRaw) + ".");
            }
            if (config.status !== "complete") {
              fail("--extend needs a completed run, and this run has status " + config.status + ". Use --lineage-mode=resume to continue an unfinished run.");
            }
            const stateBytes = fs.readFileSync(stateLogPath);
            const rows = stateBytes.toString("utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean).flatMap((line) => {
              try { return [JSON.parse(line)]; } catch { return []; }
            });
            // One run per iteration number, error and timeout rows included. The integer
            // iteration counts first and a numeric run is the fallback, as the state reader
            // counts them, because a leaf may write its run id string in run.
            const continuedFromRun = new Set(rows
              .filter((row) => row && row.type === "iteration")
              .map((row) => (Number.isInteger(row.iteration) ? row.iteration : Number(row.run)))
              .filter((run) => Number.isInteger(run))).size;
            const addedIterations = Number(extendRaw);
            const priorMaxIterations = Number(config.maxIterations) || 0;
            const priorMaxDurationMinutes = Number(config.maxDurationMinutes) || 0;
            const createdMs = Date.parse(config.createdAt);
            const elapsedMinutesAtExtension = Number.isFinite(createdMs)
              ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - createdMs) / 60000))
              : 0;
            // An unset flag renders as an empty or unexpanded value, never as digits.
            const hasMinutes = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendMinutesRaw));
            const addedMinutes = hasMinutes ? Number(extendMinutesRaw) : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            // A run with no duration budget keeps none unless --extend-minutes asks for one.
            const newMaxDurationMinutes = priorMaxDurationMinutes > 0 || hasMinutes
              ? Math.max(priorMaxDurationMinutes, elapsedMinutesAtExtension) + addedMinutes
              : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            const newMaxIterations = continuedFromRun + addedIterations;
            const sessionId = config.sessionId;
            const generation = Number(config.generation) || 1;
            const event = {
              stem: "deep_review.run_extended",
              scope: { runId: sessionId, sessionId, generation },
              data: {
                priorTailDigest: crypto.createHash("sha256").update(stateBytes).digest("hex"),
                continuedFromRun,
                extension: {
                  addedIterations,
                  priorMaxIterations,
                  newMaxIterations,
                  priorMaxDurationMinutes,
                  newMaxDurationMinutes,
                  elapsedMinutesAtExtension,
                },
              },
            };
            const next = {
              ...config,
              status: "initialized",
              maxIterations: newMaxIterations,
              maxDurationMinutes: newMaxDurationMinutes,
              lineageMode: "completed-continue",
              continuedFromRun,
            };
            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "event.json"), JSON.stringify(event));
            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(workDir, "config.json"), JSON.stringify(next, null, 2) + "\n");
            const stopPolicy = config.antiConvergence && config.antiConvergence.stopPolicy || "convergence";
            // Review has no question inbox, so under convergence an unchanged dimension set
            // can re-stop on all_dimensions_clean before the new budget is used.
            const warning = stopPolicy === "convergence"
              ? "This run stops on convergence, so it may re-stop on all_dimensions_clean right away. Pass --convergence-mode=off or use stopPolicy max-iterations to keep it running."
              : null;
            console.log(JSON.stringify({ continuedFromRun, newMaxIterations, newMaxDurationMinutes, warning }));
          ' "{state_paths.config}" "{state_paths.state_log}" "{extend}" "{extend_minutes}" "$WORK_DIR" > "$WORK_DIR/result.json" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 2; }
          node "$GATEWAY" --mode review --run-directory "$(dirname "{state_paths.state_log}")" --event-json "$WORK_DIR/event.json" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 1; }
          mv "$WORK_DIR/config.json" "{state_paths.config}" || { rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"; exit 1; }
          cat "$WORK_DIR/result.json"
          rm -rf "$WORK_DIR"
        outputs:
          - continued_from_run: "continuedFromRun from the JSON output"
          - max_iterations: "newMaxIterations from the JSON output; it replaces the command default so the loop cap and the convergence script read the same ceiling"
          - extension_pending: "true"

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '190,215p;380,400p;455,480p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md
Synthesis turns citation evidence from converged delta files into a ten-category `resource-map.md`, grouped by category and annotated with per-file citation counts. The emitter is enabled by default through `config.resource_map.emit = true`, and the `--no-resource-map` flag disables the write cleanly for a run.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/resource-map-emission.md`](../feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/resource-map-emission.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

### Completed-run extension (--extend)

#### Description

A completed deep-research run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and records one run_extended event.

#### How It Works

The run continues under lineage mode `completed-continue`, keeps the prior synthesis as a snapshot and takes `--extend-minutes=M` on the duration budget. Extension is refused with fan-out.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md`](../feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

## 3. STATE MANAGEMENT

---

### Anti-convergence floor

#### Description

Blocks convergence STOP until the configured minimum iteration floor clears.

#### How It Works

The config template defines the anti-convergence defaults and the workflow reads them before accepting a convergence STOP. A STOP candidate is overridden while `iterationCount < minIterations`; when the floor clears, the workflow emits `min_iterations_guard_pass`. `convergenceMode: "off"` disables convergence STOP while preserving hard caps, pause, and halt behavior.

#### Source Files

See [`convergence/anti-convergence-floor.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/anti-convergence-floor.md) for full implementation and test file listings.

---

### Stuck detection

`convergenceMode: "divergent"` preserves the existing convergence computation and legal-stop gates. Only `composite_converged` and `all_questions_answered` STOP reasons enter a native one-round, three-seat Council transaction. The selected state-grounded candidate becomes the next focus; hard terminal reasons remain terminal, and pivot artifacts are isolated under the loop artifact root.

#### Source Files

See [`convergence/divergent-convergence-mode.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/divergent-convergence-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)

#### Description

Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-research seeds depth-one frontier nodes from the topic, admits proposals under caps, and keeps a convergence stop open while frontier nodes stay open.

#### How It Works

`default` is the value when the flag is absent and runs unchanged. `frontier-admit.cjs` admits proposals into the node store, and the iteration cap, the floor, stuck recovery, pause, convergence off and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.

#### Source Files

See [`convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

## 5. RESEARCH OUTPUT

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '120,135p;190,205p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md
A normalized score above 0.60 nominates STOP. The legal-stop gates then check that low novelty held for the required rounds, that every key question has an evidence-backed answer, that sources are diverse and that one weak source does not carry a finding. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP. When a gate fails, the run records `blocked_stop` and continues with a recovery focus.

The shipped config floors convergence at 3 rounds, sets `stuckThreshold` to 3 and caps a run at 10 rounds and 120 minutes. Lower `--convergence` asks for more rounds. Raise it when the loop should finish sooner.

`--convergence-mode` changes the stop path. `off` disables convergence STOP while the hard caps stay active. `sliding-window` scores novelty over a bounded window. `divergent` sends an eligible legal stop into one bounded pivot round instead of synthesis, then returns to the loop.

`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

### Stop Reasons

The workflow persists one `stopReason` value when the loop ends or pauses.

| Value | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `converged` | A legal stop passed and the run may exit |

A: A shared context window fills with stale findings. Later rounds read the same material through more clutter. A fresh LEAF agent per round plus state on disk means every round reads only what the packet holds. The agent never dispatches a sub-agent or nests another loop.

**Q: Where are the findings kept?**

A: Each round writes `{spec_folder}/research/iterations/iteration-NNN.md` and a delta file under `deltas/`. Synthesis compiles the trail into `research.md`. The reducer indexes the finding set in `findings-registry.json` and draws the trend in `deep-research-dashboard.md`.

**Q: What is the difference between resume and restart?**

A: `resume` keeps the same `sessionId` and appends a `resumed` event. `restart` archives the current `research/` tree under `research_archive/`, mints a fresh `sessionId`, increments `generation` and appends a `restarted` event. `completed-continue` comes from `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session, raises the cap to the runs so far plus N, snapshots the prior synthesis and appends an `extended` event.
`fork` is declared in the lifecycle vocabulary but not wired.

**Q: Can I run each iteration through a CLI executor?**

A: Yes. Pass `--executor=<kind>`. The workflow owns executor selection. The LEAF limits still apply, including the per-round tool-call cap.
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '80,100p;128,145p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md
### Stop Decision And Counters

- The stop decision is computed once per loop pass by `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` from the packet files. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP.
- The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are derived from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals.
- Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode the workflow first asks Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.
- Each pass compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. At the limit the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.
- `deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` is the research reducer and the only writer of the dashboard.

### Expansive Depth Mode

- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions beside the seeded key questions. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before.
- `step_frontier_seed` writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`, after `step_create_config`, or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run.
- `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Admitted nodes land in `research/inbox.jsonl` with `origin: "frontier"`.
- Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first, then the least visited, then the oldest, and writes `Frontier node <id> (depth N, scope S): <question>`.
- After the `minIterations` floor an open or active node turns a soft stop into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never creates a stop and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`.
- A fan-out run deals the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across lineages and merges them back under a partition guard, so no dealt node goes unreported.

### Invocation Contract

This skill is invoked exclusively through `/deep:research:auto` or `/deep:research:confirm` -- the command YAML owns state, dispatch, convergence, and synthesis. Never simulate the loop with ad hoc shell dispatch, nested CLI loops, direct `@deep-research` Task dispatch, `/tmp` prompt files, or state outside the resolved local research packet.

| Mode | Meaning |
|------|---------|
| `new` | First run against the spec folder |
| `resume` | Continue the active lineage and append a typed `resumed` JSONL event |
| `restart` | Archive the existing research tree, mint a fresh `sessionId`, increment `generation`, and append a typed `restarted` event |

`completed-continue` is set by `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session and generation, raises the iteration cap to the runs so far plus N, and appends a typed `run_extended` event.

The deferred mode `fork` is reserved but not runtime-supported.

### Code-Graph Readiness TrustState Surface

The live code-graph readiness contract reaches four TrustState values: `live`, `stale`, `absent`, and `unavailable`. `cached`, `imported`, `rebuilt`, and `rehomed` remain declared in the shared TrustState type for compatibility, but the readiness helpers used here do not emit them today.

### Resource Map Integration

When `{spec_folder}/resource-map.md` exists at init, `resource_map_present: true` is persisted and the map is summarized into `deep-research-strategy.md` `Known Context`.

## 10A. SATURATED DIRECTIONS AND DIVERGENCE FRONTIER
- Completed pivots: 0
- Failed pivots: 0
- Audited overrides: 0
- Saturated: none yet
- Pivot lineage: none yet

## 12. KNOWN CONTEXT
[Populated during initialization from the continuity ladder and ripgrep recipes, if any prior work exists]

### Bounded Context Snapshot

Populate during initialization when the target is codebase-scoped. Keep this pointer-based and small:

- Source pointers: paths, symbols, or resource-map entries relevant to the topic.
- Reuse candidates: existing utilities, patterns, docs, or agents worth extending.
- Integration points: files or contracts the research is likely to touch.
- Constraints and risks: scope limits, stale graph or memory gaps, and known non-goals.

Do not inline full source bodies. Do not dispatch the retired standalone context loop. Use `@context` for one-shot retrieval, and use this snapshot only to seed the research loop.

Author: Michel Kerkmeester <82775228+MichelKerkmeester@users.noreply.github.com>

    fix(system-deep-loop): repair expansive review frontier defects from the pilot
    
    A live expansive review pilot over a 400-commit range found four defects on
    the frontier path. The root shared the per-node children cap, so a review seed
    admitted six depth-1 sub-targets although the workflow promises three to eight;
    the root cap is now the top of each loop's documented seed range (eight for
    review, six for research) and never below the per-node cap, which stays as it
    was. A commit-range target typed as a file list made the range string the only
    allowed path, so every depth-2 proposal pruned as irrelevant; relevance now
    intersects a range target with the files scope discovery recorded for it.
    Merged frontier nodes kept lineage-local ids, so three lineages each reported
    F-2-002; merged ids and parent links are now lineage-qualified, with the local
    id kept beside them for the partition guard. The review docs now name the
    frontier_partition key the runner records in orchestration-summary.json.
    
    Every change sits on the expansive path; both depth-mode golden suites pass.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/state/state-format.md:299:`review/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`; a default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row per `nodeId` wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit, and `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs --target review` is its only writer, for seed, admit and close.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:461:`step_frontier_seed` decomposes the review target into depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`. A merge that carries no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`, because neither is reviewable work.

Verify that `--dry-run` performs safe preflight reads and halts before dispatch, state mutation, reducer refresh, or child spawn.

### DR-065 | Completed-run extension with --extend

#### Description
Verify that `--extend=N` reopens a completed research run in the same session and generation, records one `run_extended` event, and snapshots the prior synthesis before the first new iteration.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt summary: Extend a completed deep-research fixture run by three iterations and confirm the session identifier, the single extension event, the iteration cap and the synthesis snapshot.

Expected signals: One `run_extended` event projected as one `extended` row, the original `sessionId` and generation 1 kept, `maxIterations` at the prior count plus 3, a `completed-continue` lineage, one `synthesis-v*-r*.md` snapshot, no `resumed` row and exactly three new iterations.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DR-065](../manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/extend-completed-research-run.md)

### DR-068 | Expansive depth flag and default byte identity

#### Description
Verify that `--depth-mode` defaults to `default`, that `--depth-mode=expansive` is the only opt-in value and that a default run matches the pre-change baseline byte for byte, with the expansive-only agent paragraph as the only CLI lineage difference.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt summary: Check that --depth-mode defaults to default and that a research run without the flag matches the pre-change baseline byte for byte. Name any byte that differs. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.

Expected signals: The flag row names `default` as the value when the flag is absent, the config carries `"depthMode": "default"`, the golden suite passes 12 of 12 tests, and the only paragraph the comparison removes is the expansive-only persona paragraph in the CLI lineage prompt.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DR-068](../manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md)

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/initialization.md:27:Lineage handling is narrower than some older drafts. The runtime supports `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue`. Resume keeps the same session identifier and appends a typed `resumed` event. Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, raises `maxIterations` by N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md:29:The iteration cap rises by `N` over the prior count. `--extend-minutes=M` is valid only with `--extend`. The new duration budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs.

          - loop_counters_json: "Tool loop_counters_json object: stuckCount and consecutiveFailureCount, both rebuilt from the state log on this pass"
          - stuck_count: "loop_counters_json.stuckCount"
          - consecutive_failure_count: "loop_counters_json.consecutiveFailureCount"

        bookkeeping_log: '{"type":"event","event":"graph_convergence","mode":"research","run":{current_iteration},"decision":{graph_decision_json},"signals":{graph_signals_json},"blockers":{graph_blockers_json},"timestamp":"{ISO_8601_NOW}","sessionId":"{config.lineage.sessionId}","generation":{config.lineage.generation}}'

      step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the loop config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out every iteration whose status is thought, error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap bypasses every guard and veto; failed guards are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every question is answered (all_questions_answered) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged).
          5. if iteration_count < effective_min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached)
          6. a failed quality guard turns the candidate into CONTINUE (qualityGuardFailed)
          7. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            gate_convergence_pass = (stop_decision.graphDecision == "STOP_ALLOWED")
            gate_convergence_score = graph_convergence_score
            gate_key_question_coverage_pass = no blocker named questionCoverage
            gate_evidence_density_pass = no blocker named sourceDiversity or evidenceDepth
            gate_evidence_density_sources = graph_signals_json.sourceDiversity or 0
            gate_hotspot_saturation_pass = true
            recovery_strategy = first blocker reason/message, else "Collect deeper graph evidence before stopping."
            min_iterations_guard_blocked = (stop_decision.reasonDetail == "minIterationsNotReached")
            min_iterations_guard_pass = true when effective_min_iterations > 1 and iteration_count >= effective_min_iterations and min_iterations_guard_already_passed == false
          If stop_decision is missing from the step_graph_convergence output, do not decide by hand: halt and report the script output.
          When stop_decision.reasonDetail == "qualityGuardFailed", log: "Quality guard failed: {blocked_by_csv}. Continuing."
          Segment-aware convergence is experimental/reference-only; live workflow uses the current session as a single segment.
        outputs:
          - decision: "STOP | BLOCKED | STUCK_RECOVERY | CONTINUE"
          - reason: "stop_decision.stopReason when set, else stop_decision.reasonDetail"
          - reason_detail: "stop_decision.reasonDetail, the fine cause behind the decision"
          - weighted_stop_score: "stop_decision.weightedStopScore, for diagnostics only"
          - stop_blocked: "Boolean true when stop_decision.decision == BLOCKED"
          - blocked_by_json: "JSON array of graph blocker names when decision == BLOCKED"
