# ────────
# STATE FILE PATHS
# ────────
# ────────
# CANONICAL STATE WRITE MECHANISM
# ────────
state_write_protocol:
  mechanism: "append-gateway"
  command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/append-mode-event.cjs --mode review --run-directory {artifact_dir} --event-json <record file>"
  applies_to: "every append_to_jsonl and append_jsonl directive in this workflow"
  refusal_handling: "halt on exit 2; never fall back to a direct file write. Exit 1 on a row that is still routed through the gateway is a workflow defect and halts; the pinned_bookkeeping rows are recorded through bookkeeping_log and are never routed."
  pinned_bookkeeping:
    events: [config_warning, lock_released, pivot_confirm_accepted, manualStop, pivot_override_accepted, maxDurationReached]
    directive: "bookkeeping_log"
    handling: "These rows are workflow bookkeeping that the review ledger schema pins to the legacy path: they have no lossless review-event target, so the gateway would refuse them with exit 1 and write nothing. They are never sent to the gateway. The step prints the row in its log line and continues; none of them is a halt condition."
  contract: |
    The directives below specify WHAT to record; this block specifies the mechanism. The gateway
    authorizes the write against the mode's durable authority record, serialises it behind the
    ledger fence, returns a receipt, and refreshes state_log from the ledger. Exit 0 means the
    record is durable in the ledger and the refreshed state_log carries it. Exit 2 means it was
    refused, and the refusal names the check that failed.

    Writing state_log directly bypasses ordering, authorization, and the receipt. Two writers
    can then interleave with no shared ordering, and a record nobody authorized carries no
    receipt, so a later reader cannot tell an accepted record from an invented one. A refusal
    is a halt, never a reason to write the file directly.

    There is no direct-append exception. state_log is a projection the gateway rebuilds from
    the ledger, so a row written beside it is dropped by the next refresh: the migration
    marker, the recovery baseline, the iteration-error row and the claim-adjudication gate
    row are canonical ledger events like every other record.

state_paths:
  # Template paths document the canonical packet layout. Runtime resolution happens via
  # step_resolve_artifact_root plus the shared resolver, so root specs keep
  # root-local review/ and child phases keep local review/ packet dirs.
  # Canonical root-spec write targets remain review/deep-review-config.json,
  # review/deep-review-state.jsonl, and review/deep-review-findings-registry.json.
  packet_dir: "{artifact_dir}"
  local_packet_dir: "{spec_folder}/review"
  legacy_root_report: "{spec_folder}/review-report.md"
  config: "{artifact_dir}/deep-review-config.json"
  state_log: "{artifact_dir}/deep-review-state.jsonl"
  findings_registry: "{artifact_dir}/deep-review-findings-registry.json"
  strategy: "{artifact_dir}/deep-review-strategy.md"
  dashboard: "{artifact_dir}/deep-review-dashboard.md"
  pause_sentinel: "{artifact_dir}/.deep-review-pause"
  lock_file: "{artifact_dir}/.deep-review.lock"
  prompt_dir: "{artifact_dir}/prompts"
  iteration_dir: "{artifact_dir}/iterations"
  iteration_pattern: "{artifact_dir}/iterations/iteration-{NNN}.md"
  # Per-iteration structured delta stream — complement to the canonical state_log
  # append. MUST contain at least one record with `type === "iteration"`; variants
  # like `"iteration_delta"` are silently dropped by the reducer.
  delta_dir: "{artifact_dir}/deltas"
  delta_pattern: "{artifact_dir}/deltas/iter-{NNN}.jsonl"
  review_output: "{artifact_dir}/review-report.md"
  resource_map_output: "{artifact_dir}/resource-map.md"
  archive_root: "{artifact_archive_root}"

---

### Completed-run extension (--extend)

#### Description

A completed deep-review run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and keeps the prior review report as a snapshot before re-synthesis.

#### How It Works

The run continues under lineage mode `completed-continue`, keeps the prior review report as a snapshot before re-synthesis and takes `--extend-minutes=M` on the duration budget. Extension is refused with fan-out.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md`](loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

### Review minIterations floor

#### Description

A review run with a configured minIterations floor returns CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached until its iteration count reaches the floor, so a review never stops before the floor.

#### How It Works

`convergence.cjs` returns `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached` below the floor, and the command's fixed stop order checks the floor before the gates. The shipped default is 2.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/min-iterations-floor.md`](loop-lifecycle/min-iterations-floor.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)

#### Description

Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-review seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target, keeps node status in review/frontier-nodes.jsonl, and holds a review stop while nodes stay open.

#### How It Works

Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, open nodes count as `searchDebt`, and the divergent pivot waits for a terminal frontier. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md`](loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '2430,2462p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs
try { row = JSON.parse(line); } catch { continue; }
    if (!row || typeof row !== 'object' || typeof row.nodeId !== 'string' || !row.nodeId) continue;
    byId.set(row.nodeId, row);
  }
  return [...byId.values()].sort((left, right) => left.nodeId.localeCompare(right.nodeId));
}

const FRONTIER_STATUSES = ['open', 'active', 'answered', 'blocked', 'exhausted', 'abstained', 'pruned'];

// A node the store still holds open is answered once its question resolves in
// the registry, so the status is derived here instead of written back.
function buildFrontierProjection(frontierInput, keyedQuestions) {
  const resolvedByNode = new Map();
  for (const question of keyedQuestions) {
    if (question.inboxId) resolvedByNode.set(question.inboxId, question.resolved);
  }
  const nodes = frontierInput.nodes.map((node) => {
    const stillOpen = node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active';
    const status = stillOpen && resolvedByNode.get(node.nodeId) === true ? 'answered' : node.status;
    return {
      nodeId: node.nodeId,
      parentId: node.parentId ?? null,
      depth: node.depth,
      scope: node.scope ?? null,
      question: node.question ?? null,
      status,
      reason: status === node.status ? (node.reason ?? null) : null,
      iteration: node.iteration ?? null,
    };
  });
  const stats = { total: nodes.length };
  for (const status of FRONTIER_STATUSES) stats[status] = nodes.filter((node) => node.status === status).length;
  return { nodes, stats, ...(frontierInput.budget ? { budget: frontierInput.budget } : {}) };
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '70,100p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/state/state-format.md
- a `{basename(spec_folder)}-pt-NN` subfolder is allocated only when prior content already exists for a non-matching target;
- continuation runs reuse the existing matching packet.

This avoids an unnecessary `pt-01` wrapper on first runs.

**Example (first run on a child phase):** `.../026-graph.../019-system-hardening/001-initial-research/004-desc-regen/` writes to `004-desc-regen/research/` directly.

**Example (subsequent run with prior content for a different target):** `004-desc-regen/research/004-desc-regen-pt-02/` (pt-NN allocated as a sibling to the prior content).

### Frontier Node Store (expansive depth only)

`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row for a node wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit. `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` is the only writer.

Statuses are `open`, `active`, `answered`, `blocked`, `exhausted`, `abstained` and `pruned`. A frontier question resolves when its node reaches a terminal status.

The reducer projects the store as `registry.frontier {nodes, stats, budget}`, applies the depth budget so an unspent share rolls down to the next depth only, closes a spent band's open nodes as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and adds a `## 6B. FRONTIER` dashboard section.

---

## 3. OWNERSHIP MODEL

| Owner | Writes |
|-------|--------|
| Agent iteration | `iterations/iteration-NNN.md`, JSONL iteration/event append, optional progressive synthesis contribution |
| Workflow reducer | strategy machine-owned sections, findings registry, dashboard |
| Workflow synthesis | `research.md`, lifecycle snapshots |
| Spec anchoring protocol | bounded `spec.md` seed/context/fenced findings block |

The reducer is the source of truth for derived state. Manual edits to reducer-owned outputs are overwritten on the next refresh.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt summary: Check that deep-research runs the findings gate and the focusAlignment guard on the fields each leaf writes, on every default research run. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.

Expected signals: A projected row that claims findings with nothing enumerated returns `findings_not_enumerated`. `focusAlignment` fails on an off-focus latest record and passes after a later aligned iteration. The projection carries the ten leaf keys.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DR-067](../manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/focus-alignment-and-findings-gate-read-leaf-fields.md)

### DR-070 | Frontier admission prunes each proposal with its reason

#### Description
Verify that each failing admission check prunes a leaf proposal with that check's own reason, and that only an admitted proposal reaches the research inbox.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt summary: Check that every frontier proposal that fails an admission check is pruned with that check's reason, and that only the admitted proposal reaches the research inbox with origin frontier. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.

Expected signals: The helper returns the six reasons `relevance`, `dedupe`, `depth`, `fanout`, `budget` and `cycle`, the seven prune rows pass, the admitted proposal writes one inbox row with `origin: "frontier"`, and the pruned proposal writes a store row and no inbox row.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DR-070](../manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/frontier-admission-prunes-with-reasons.md)

### DR-071 | Frontier focus picks the shallowest open node

#### Description
Verify that Next Focus under the expansive mode picks the open node at the lowest depth, that one visit with that focus leaves its question open and that a reworded focus still counts as a visit.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt summary: Check that expansive Next Focus picks the shallowest open frontier node, that one visit with that focus leaves its question open, and that a reworded focus that keeps the node marker still counts as a visit. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.

Expected signals: The focus case chooses a depth-one node over a depth-two node and leaves its question open after the visit, the reworded-visit case spends the node as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and the reducer matches the `Frontier node` marker anywhere in the focus.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DR-071](../manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/frontier-focus-picks-shallowest-open-node.md)

### Custom review dimensions

#### Description

A review run accepts configured custom dimensions after the four canonical ones, tracks them in dimension coverage, and lets a covered custom dimension reach the all-dimensions-clean stop.

#### How It Works

Custom names are ordered after the four canonical dimensions, judged by their own name and not required for severity coverage.

#### Source Files

See [`review-dimensions/custom-review-dimensions.md`](review-dimensions/custom-review-dimensions.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

### Workflow Flag Surface

The argument hint names the invocation shape; the whole flag surface is here. Every flag below
is a workflow input, never an execution mode, and the presentation asset owns how each one binds
into `config`.
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '96,104p' .skilled/commands/deep/research.md | cut -c1-160
into `config`.

| Flag | Value | Effect |
|------|-------|--------|
| `--spec-folder` | `PATH` | Binds the packet that owns this run's writes. |
| `--max-iterations` | `N` | Hard ceiling on loop iterations. |
| `--convergence` | `N` | Threshold the loop must clear before convergence may stop it. |
| `--convergence-mode` | `default` \| `off` \| `sliding-window` \| `divergent` | `default` keeps the anti-convergence `minIterations` floor. `off` disables conv
| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n "Q: |A: " .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md | tail -12
185:**Q: How does convergence decide to stop?**
187:A: A low novelty score only nominates STOP. The weighted score must clear 0.60 before a STOP is nominated. Then the legal-stop gates check novelty over the required rounds, question coverage, source diversity and graph evidence. A blocked gate writes a `blocked_stop` event and the loop continues with a recovery focus. The full decision order is in [the convergence reference](./references/convergence/convergence.md).
189:**Q: Why does each iteration start a fresh agent?**
191:A: A shared context window fills with stale findings. Later rounds read the same material through more clutter. A fresh LEAF agent per round plus state on disk means every round reads only what the packet holds. The agent never dispatches a sub-agent or nests another loop.
193:**Q: Where are the findings kept?**
195:A: Each round writes `{spec_folder}/research/iterations/iteration-NNN.md` and a delta file under `deltas/`. Synthesis compiles the trail into `research.md`. The reducer indexes the finding set in `findings-registry.json` and draws the trend in `deep-research-dashboard.md`.
197:**Q: What is the difference between resume and restart?**
199:A: `resume` keeps the same `sessionId` and appends a `resumed` event. `restart` archives the current `research/` tree under `research_archive/`, mints a fresh `sessionId`, increments `generation` and appends a `restarted` event. `completed-continue` comes from `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session, raises the cap to the runs so far plus N, snapshots the prior synthesis and appends an `extended` event.
202:**Q: Can I run each iteration through a CLI executor?**
204:A: Yes. Pass `--executor=<kind>`. The workflow owns executor selection. The LEAF limits still apply, including the per-round tool-call cap.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n "extend|depth-mode|expansive|frontier" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md
199:### Completed-run extension (--extend)
203:A completed deep-research run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and records one run_extended event.
207:The run continues under lineage mode `completed-continue`, keeps the prior synthesis as a snapshot and takes `--extend-minutes=M` on the duration budget. Extension is refused with fan-out.
463:### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)
467:Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-research seeds depth-one frontier nodes from the topic, admits proposals under caps, and keeps a convergence stop open while frontier nodes stay open.
471:`default` is the value when the flag is absent and runs unchanged. `frontier-admit.cjs` admits proposals into the node store, and the iteration cap, the floor, stuck recovery, pause, convergence off and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.
475:See [`convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md`](../feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.
491:`progressiveSynthesis` defaults to `true`, so the agent may create or extend `research/research.md` during each iteration while the workflow still owns the final cleanup pass. If the flag is disabled, iteration work leaves the file alone and the synthesis phase creates it from scratch, but in both cases `research/research.md` stays the canonical workflow-owned research output.

 # Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)
@@ -15,6 +15,8 @@ version: 1.0.0.0
 
 `--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A run without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` turns on a research frontier: the run seeds depth-one nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as nodes under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps, and holds a convergence stop while frontier nodes stay open. The live pilot runs that would show this mode on real topics are still open, so this page describes the shipped code and its tests and records no pilot result.
 
+---
+
 ## 2. HOW IT WORKS
 
 ### Seeding And Admission

 # Completed-run extension (--extend)
@@ -16,6 +16,8 @@ version: 1.0.0.0
 
 `--extend=N` reopens a completed research run in the same session and generation and adds `N` iterations. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event, and keeps the prior synthesis as `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. `--extend-minutes=M` adds minutes to the duration budget of the same extension.

144:**Convergence window.** Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new to cover re-stops on `all_questions_answered` at the first convergence check. The extension step prints a warning when this is likely; to keep the run going, pass `--convergence-mode=off`, use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`, or add questions to `research/inbox.jsonl`.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n "raises .maxIterations. by N" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/initialization.md; echo ---; rg -n "Command 1 prints 13 lines" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/frontier-admission-prunes-with-reasons.md; echo ---; rg -n "15 passed \| 64 skipped" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/convergence-and-recovery/frontier-open-veto-holds-soft-stop.md; echo ---; rg -n "Command 1 prints|Command 4 prints" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/fanout/fanout-frontier-partition-round-robin.md
27:Lineage handling is narrower than some older drafts. The runtime supports `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue`. Resume keeps the same session identifier and appends a typed `resumed` event. Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, raises `maxIterations` by N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.
---
57:Command 1 prints 13 lines. Every one of the six reason strings appears, because some reasons are returned from more than one guard. Command 2 prints `Tests  7 passed | 17 skipped (24)`, with seven prune rows: two for `relevance`, and one each for `dedupe`, `depth`, `fanout`, `budget` and `cycle`. Command 3 prints `Tests  1 passed | 23 skipped (24)`, and that case asserts one inbox row with `origin: "frontier"` and one open store row. Command 4 prints `Tests  1 passed | 23 skipped (24)`, and that case asserts a store row with no inbox line.

Command 3 prints the veto branch that returns `CONTINUE` with `frontierOpen`, and the research facts line that counts open and active nodes.
